# playgta5 Source Code
This code is grabbed from playgta5.com before it got took down. You can run this locally or make it public hehe.

# Credits
Shoutout to Sebas Furbastian on Telegram for scrapping this code. Idk what is his GitHub but here's the Telegram and X.

X: @Sebas_Kitten

Telegram: https://t.me/reGRANDTA

Discord: https://discord.gg/hrbcPjjZfj


# Disclaimer
I don't host the `.\mirror` folder since it has copyrighted content from Rockstar Games. Please find the files by yourself.

No copyrighted file is included in this repo. If Rockstar Games or any affiliated group think this repo has copyright infringement things, email me at shadany7824@gmail.com for me to took it down.

And don't email me for asking the mirror folder. I will not reply to the email.

# How to use
1. Download the ZIP or just `git clone` it. It should have all this file and folders.
<p align="center">
  <img src="media/Screenshot 2026-10-07 172103.png" alt="playgta5 SC">
</p>

2. Paste your `.mirror` folder at your desired path. (It should have 19.7 GB of file size. Check the pic below.)
<p align="center">
  <img src="media/Screenshot 2026-10-07 172216.png" alt="Folder Properties">
</p>
<p align="center">
  <img src="media/Screenshot 2026-10-07 172718.png" alt="Folder Properties">
</p>
<p align="center">
  <img src="media/Screenshot 2026-10-07 172758.png" alt="Folder Properties">
</p>

3. Run the `Launch-Local.cmd` and it will automatically open the URL at `http://localhost:8000/`.

4. Voila!

# Requirement
Scripts require standard-library Python 3.11 or newer. The bundled Python path
in the commands above is specific to the original PC; the portable ZIP instead
provides `runtime\python.exe` and the double-click launcher.
Completed files and `.part` transfers are retained for resumption. Final checks
cover inventory sizes, runtime hash samples, WASM signature, HTTP isolation,
range reads and both batch formats. Actual gameplay requires separate browser
validation. A public client snapshot is not the site's original development repository.

To resume with the current uncapped settings, append `--workers 32 --rate-mib 0`
to the downloader command. Content lengths from HTTP take precedence over the
source manifest when it is stale; mismatches are recorded explicitly.

FILE: append_investigation.py
================================================
"""Append newly created evidence to the existing portable ZIP, then refresh SHA256."""
import hashlib, json, time, zipfile
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ARCHIVE = ROOT / 'playgta5-offline-2026-10-06.zip'
PREFIX = 'playgta5-offline/'
NAMES = ['INVESTIGATION.md', 'ENGINE_INVESTIGATION.md', 'inspect_assets.py',
         'engine_static_evidence.py', 'append_investigation.py', 'snapshot/asset-evidence.json',
         'snapshot/engine-evidence.json', 'snapshot/domain-rdap.json']

def main():
    records = []
    original_entries = {}
    with zipfile.ZipFile(ARCHIVE, 'a', compression=zipfile.ZIP_STORED, allowZip64=True) as archive:
        original_entries = {i.filename: (i.CRC, i.file_size, i.header_offset) for i in archive.infolist()}
        for name in NAMES:
            if PREFIX + name in original_entries:
                raise ValueError('Already appended; refusing duplicate archive entries: ' + name)
            body = (ROOT / name).read_bytes()
            archive.writestr(PREFIX + name, body)
            records.append({'path': name, 'bytes': len(body), 'sha256': hashlib.sha256(body).hexdigest()})
        manifest = json.dumps({'scope': 'Evidence appended after the original asset bundle; original entries unchanged.',
                               'files': records}, indent=2).encode()
        archive.writestr(PREFIX + 'snapshot/investigation-manifest.json', manifest)
    (ROOT / 'snapshot/investigation-manifest.json').write_bytes(manifest)
    with zipfile.ZipFile(ARCHIVE) as archive:
        current = {i.filename: (i.CRC, i.file_size, i.header_offset) for i in archive.infolist()}
        assert all(current[name] == value for name, value in original_entries.items())
        for rec in records:
            body = archive.read(PREFIX + rec['path'])
            assert hashlib.sha256(body).hexdigest() == rec['sha256']
        assert archive.read(PREFIX + 'snapshot/investigation-manifest.json') == manifest
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    read_bytes = 0
    last = time.monotonic()
    with ARCHIVE.open('rb') as source:
        while chunk := source.read(8 * 1024 * 1024):
            digest.update(chunk)
            read_bytes += len(chunk)
            if time.monotonic() - last > 10:
                print('Final ZIP SHA256: %.1f%%' % (read_bytes / ARCHIVE.stat().st_size * 100), flush=True)
                last = time.monotonic()
    (ROOT / (ARCHIVE.name + '.sha256')).write_text(digest.hexdigest() + '  ' + ARCHIVE.name + '\n')
    report_file = ROOT / 'snapshot/package-report.json'
    report = json.loads(report_file.read_text())
    report.update(bytes=ARCHIVE.stat().st_size, sha256=digest.hexdigest(), archive_entries=len(current),
                  investigation_files_added=len(records) + 1,
                  appended_evidence_validation='Original entry CRC/size/header offsets unchanged; all new entries read and SHA256 checked.')
    report_file.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    (ROOT / 'snapshot/package-status.json').write_text(json.dumps({'phase': 'complete', **report}, indent=2))
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2), flush=True)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()



================================================
FILE: build_portable_zip.py
================================================
"""Build a ZIP64 portable bundle and hash source files as they enter the archive."""
import hashlib, json, time, zipfile
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
PREFIX = 'playgta5-offline'
OUTPUT = ROOT / 'playgta5-offline-2026-10-06.zip'

class HashWriter:
    """Non-seekable ZIP output: the hash covers every byte exactly once."""
    def __init__(self, raw):
        self.raw = raw
        self.digest = hashlib.sha256()
        self.position = 0
    def write(self, data):
        n = self.raw.write(data)
        self.digest.update(data[:n])
        self.position += n
        return n
    def tell(self):
        return self.position
    def flush(self):
        self.raw.flush()

def main():
    expected = {'mirror/playgta5.com/' + r['path'].lstrip('/'): r['sha256']
                for r in json.loads((ROOT / 'snapshot/manifest-sha256.json').read_text())}
    expected.update({r['path']: r['sha256'] for r in
        json.loads((ROOT / 'snapshot/runtime-manifest.json').read_text())['files']})
    files = []
    for name in ['mirror', 'runtime', 'snapshot']:
        for path in (ROOT / name).rglob('*'):
            if path.is_file() and '__pycache__' not in path.parts and path.suffix != '.part' and path.name not in {'package-manifest.json', 'package-report.json', 'package-status.json'}:
                files.append(path)
    for name in ['README.md', 'Launch-Local.cmd', 'Start-Local.ps1', 'serve_local.py', 'verify_snapshot.py',
                 'mirror_site.py', 'bundle_runtime.py', 'build_portable_zip.py', 'homepage.html',
                 'loader.js', 'game.js', 'io_worker.js', 'wgpu_worker.js', 'headers.txt',
                 'data-manifest.json', 'shader-index.json', 'public_discovery.py']:
        files.append(ROOT / name)
    files.sort(key=lambda p: str(p.relative_to(ROOT)))
    total = sum(p.stat().st_size for p in files)
    processed = 0
    records = []
    start = time.monotonic()
    last_report = start
    temporary = OUTPUT.with_suffix('.zip.part')
    print('Packaging %d files, %d bytes, stored ZIP64' % (len(files), total), flush=True)
    with temporary.open('wb') as raw:
        writer = HashWriter(raw)
        with zipfile.ZipFile(writer, 'w', compression=zipfile.ZIP_STORED, allowZip64=True) as archive:
            for path in files:
                relative = str(path.relative_to(ROOT)).replace('\\', '/')
                info = zipfile.ZipInfo.from_file(path, PREFIX + '/' + relative)
                digest = hashlib.sha256()
                size = 0
                with path.open('rb') as source, archive.open(info, 'w', force_zip64=True) as target:
                    while chunk := source.read(1024 * 1024):
                        digest.update(chunk)
                        target.write(chunk)
                        size += len(chunk)
                        processed += len(chunk)
                        if time.monotonic() - last_report >= 10:
                            status = {'phase': 'packaging', 'processed_bytes': processed, 'total_bytes': total,
                                'percent': round(processed / total * 100, 1), 'current_file': relative}
                            (ROOT / 'snapshot/package-status.json').write_text(json.dumps(status, indent=2))
                            print(json.dumps(status), flush=True)
                            last_report = time.monotonic()
                sha = digest.hexdigest()
                if relative in expected and expected[relative] != sha:
                    raise ValueError('Source hash changed: ' + relative)
                records.append({'path': relative, 'bytes': size, 'sha256': sha})
            package_manifest = {'root_folder': PREFIX, 'files': records,
                'scope': 'All listed files hashed while packaging; this manifest does not list itself.'}
            manifest_bytes = json.dumps(package_manifest, indent=2).encode()
            archive.writestr(PREFIX + '/snapshot/package-manifest.json', manifest_bytes)
        archive_hash = writer.digest.hexdigest()
    temporary.replace(OUTPUT)
    (ROOT / 'snapshot/package-manifest.json').write_bytes(manifest_bytes)
    with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT) as archive:
        assert len(archive.infolist()) == len(records) + 1
        assert archive.read(PREFIX + '/snapshot/package-manifest.json') == manifest_bytes
        assert archive.read(PREFIX + '/Launch-Local.cmd') == (ROOT / 'Launch-Local.cmd').read_bytes()
        assert archive.read(PREFIX + '/runtime/python312._pth') == (ROOT / 'runtime/python312._pth').read_bytes()
        for rec, info in zip(records, archive.infolist()):
            assert info.filename == PREFIX + '/' + rec['path'] and info.file_size == rec['bytes']
    report = {'archive': OUTPUT.name, 'bytes': OUTPUT.stat().st_size, 'sha256': archive_hash,
        'archive_entries': len(records) + 1, 'source_bytes': total,
        'source_sha256_verified_against_inventory': len(expected), 'compression': 'stored ZIP64',
        'seconds': round(time.monotonic() - start, 1),
        'validation': 'All source SHA-256 checked during packing; archive directory, lengths, manifest, launcher and isolated runtime configuration checked afterward.'}
    (ROOT / 'snapshot/package-report.json').write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    (ROOT / (OUTPUT.name + '.sha256')).write_text(archive_hash + '  ' + OUTPUT.name + '\n', encoding='ascii')
    (ROOT / 'snapshot/package-status.json').write_text(json.dumps({'phase': 'complete', **report}, indent=2))
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2), flush=True)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()



================================================
FILE: bundle_runtime.py
================================================
"""Build a portable standard-library Python runtime from the local Codex bundle."""
import hashlib, json, shutil, sys, zipfile
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
SOURCE = Path(sys.executable).parent
TARGET = ROOT / 'runtime'
TARGET.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
tag = 'python%d%d' % sys.version_info[:2]
for name in ['python.exe', 'python3.dll', tag + '.dll', 'vcruntime140.dll', 'vcruntime140_1.dll', 'LICENSE.txt']:
    shutil.copyfile(SOURCE / name, TARGET / name)
shutil.copytree(SOURCE / 'DLLs', TARGET / 'DLLs', dirs_exist_ok=True,
                ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns('__pycache__', '*.pyc', '*.pdb'))
with zipfile.ZipFile(TARGET / (tag + '.zip'), 'w', compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED, compresslevel=6) as archive:
    for path in sorted((SOURCE / 'Lib').rglob('*')):
        rel = path.relative_to(SOURCE / 'Lib')
        if path.is_file() and not any(part in {'site-packages', '__pycache__', 'test', 'tests'} for part in rel.parts) and path.suffix != '.pyc':
            archive.write(path, str(rel))
# An isolated path avoids depending on the user's Python install or site packages.
(TARGET / (tag + '._pth')).write_text(tag + '.zip\nDLLs\n.\n..\n', encoding='ascii')
records = []
for path in sorted(TARGET.rglob('*')):
    if path.is_file():
        records.append({'path': str(path.relative_to(ROOT)).replace('\\', '/'), 'bytes': path.stat().st_size,
                        'sha256': hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()})
(ROOT / 'snapshot/runtime-manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps({'python_version': sys.version,
    'source': str(SOURCE), 'scope': 'Python executable, DLLs and standard library; third-party packages excluded',
    'files': records}, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
print('Portable runtime:', len(records), 'files;', sum(r['bytes'] for r in records), 'bytes')

FILE: engine_static_evidence.py
================================================
"""Static, bounded evidence extraction from the game WebAssembly and glue files."""
import hashlib, json, re
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent; BUILD=ROOT/'mirror/playgta5.com/b/8b0b5899ed'
WASM=BUILD/'game.wasm'; JS=BUILD/'game.js'
b=WASM.read_bytes()
def uleb(pos):
    n=0; shift=0
    while True:
        x=b[pos]; pos+=1; n|=(x&127)<<shift
        if not x&128:return n,pos
        shift+=7
        if shift>70: raise ValueError('invalid LEB')
def name(pos):
    n,pos=uleb(pos); return b[pos:pos+n].decode('utf-8','replace'),pos+n
def skip_limits(pos):
    flag=b[pos];pos+=1; _,pos=uleb(pos)
    if flag&1: _,pos=uleb(pos)
    return pos
def skip_type(pos, kind):
    if kind==0: return skip_limits(pos)
    if kind==1: return b[pos+1] and skip_limits(pos+1) # never used
    if kind==2: return skip_limits(pos)
    if kind==3: return skip_limits(pos)
    raise ValueError(kind)
sections=[]; custom=[]; imports=[]; exports=[]; memories=[]; pos=8
sec_names={0:'custom',1:'type',2:'import',3:'function',4:'table',5:'memory',6:'global',7:'export',8:'start',9:'element',10:'code',11:'data',12:'data_count',13:'tag'}
while pos<len(b):
    start=pos; sid=b[pos]; pos+=1; sz,pos=uleb(pos); payload=pos; end=pos+sz
    if end>len(b): raise ValueError('section exceeds file')
    sections.append({'id':sid,'name':sec_names.get(sid,'unknown'),'offset':start,'payload_offset':payload,'payload_bytes':sz,'end_offset':end})
    if sid==0:
        n,p=name(payload); custom.append({'name':n,'section_offset':start,'payload_offset':p,'payload_bytes':end-p})
    elif sid==2:
        n,p=uleb(payload)
        for _ in range(n):
            mod,p=name(p); field,p=name(p); kind=b[p]; p+=1; desc=p
            if kind==0: index,p=uleb(p); detail={'type_index':index}
            elif kind==1: elem=b[p];p+=1;p=skip_limits(p); detail={'element_type':elem}
            elif kind==2: p=skip_limits(p); detail={'limits':True}
            elif kind==3: vt=b[p]; mut=b[p+1];p+=2; detail={'value_type':vt,'mutable':bool(mut)}
            elif kind==4: index,p=uleb(p); detail={'type_index':index}
            else: raise ValueError('unknown import kind')
            imports.append({'module':mod,'field':field,'kind':kind,'offset':desc-1,**detail})
    elif sid==5:
        n,p=uleb(payload)
        for _ in range(n):
            at=p; p=skip_limits(p); memories.append({'offset':at})
    elif sid==7:
        n,p=uleb(payload)
        for _ in range(n):
            nm,p=name(p); kind=b[p];p+=1; ix,p=uleb(p); exports.append({'name':nm,'kind':kind,'index':ix})
    pos=end

# Printable runs are indexed only when they carry an explicit build/engine/compiler/authorship clue.
terms=re.compile(r'(emscripten|clang|llvm|wasm|webgpu|d3d11|dxgi|rage|rockstar|grand theft|gta[ _-]?v|gta5|\.cpp|\.cc|\.cxx|\.h(?:pp)?(?:\x00|$)|/[A-Za-z0-9_. -]+\.(?:cpp|cc|cxx|h|hpp))',re.I)
hits=[]
for m in re.finditer(rb'[ -~]{4,}',b):
    s=m.group().decode('ascii','replace')
    if terms.search(s): hits.append({'offset':m.start(),'text':s[:1000]})
hits=hits[:300]
priority_patterns={
 'game_version': rb'(?:GTA ?V|Grand Theft Auto|GTA5)[ -~]{0,100}',
 'rockstar_or_rage': rb'(?:Rockstar|RAGE|RageNet|ragenet)[ -~]{0,140}',
 'source_paths': rb'(?:[A-Za-z]:[\\/]|(?:/|\\\\)[A-Za-z0-9_. -]+[\\/])[ -~]{0,180}\\.(?:cpp|cc|cxx|hpp|h)',
 'compiler_markers': rb'(?:clang|llvm|emscripten)[ -~]{0,120}',
 'version_or_build_markers': rb'(?:version|build)[ -~]{0,140}',
}
priority_hits={}
for label,pat in priority_patterns.items():
    vals=[]
    for m in re.finditer(pat,b,re.I):
        vals.append({'offset':m.start(),'text':m.group().decode('ascii','replace')[:500]})
        if len(vals)>=80: break
    priority_hits[label]=vals

# The standard name custom section preserves function symbols. Traverse it all,
# but retain only bounded category samples.
name_summary={k:{'count':0,'samples':[]} for k in ('rage_native','version_or_build','product_id','pc_dx11','console_backend','browser_port')}
name_rules={
 'rage_native': re.compile(r'\brage::',re.I),
 'version_or_build': re.compile(r'(?:version|build|patch)',re.I),
 'product_id': re.compile(r'(?:gta(?:v|5)|grand.?theft)',re.I),
 'pc_dx11': re.compile(r'(?:d3d11|dxgi|direct3d|win32|windows|\bpc\b)',re.I),
 'console_backend': re.compile(r'(?:xenon|orbis|durango|prospero|ps3|ps4|ps5)',re.I),
 'browser_port': re.compile(r'(?:emscripten|wasm|httpfs|wgpu|webgpu)',re.I),
}
for c in custom:
    if c['name']!='name': continue
    p=c['payload_offset']; end=c['payload_offset']+c['payload_bytes']
    while p<end:
        subid=b[p]; p+=1; size,p=uleb(p); subend=p+size
        if subid==1:
            count,p2=uleb(p)
            for _ in range(count):
                idx,p2=uleb(p2); nm,p2=name(p2)
                for label,rule in name_rules.items():
                    if rule.search(nm):
                        name_summary[label]['count']+=1
                        if len(name_summary[label]['samples'])<30:
                            name_summary[label]['samples'].append({'function_index':idx,'name':nm})
        p=subend

# Scan only the Wasm data section for product/platform/build-value clues. Generic
# words are retained only if they contain a value-like suffix or product/platform term.
data_sec=next(x for x in sections if x['name']=='data')
data_blob=b[data_sec['payload_offset']:data_sec['end_offset']]
data_categories={k:{'count':0,'samples':[]} for k in ('source_paths','version_or_build_values','product_identifiers','pc_dx11','console_backend')}
data_rules={
 'source_paths': re.compile(r'^[A-Za-z]:[\\/].+',re.I),
 'version_or_build_values': re.compile(r'\b(?:build|version|patch)\b.*(?:\d|0x[0-9a-f]+)',re.I),
 'product_identifiers': re.compile(r'(?:gta(?:v|5)|grand.?theft|rockstar)',re.I),
 'pc_dx11': re.compile(r'(?:d3d11|dxgi|direct3d|win32|windows|platform_pc|\bpc\b)',re.I),
 'console_backend': re.compile(r'(?:xenon|orbis|durango|prospero|ps3|ps4|ps5)',re.I),
}
for m in re.finditer(rb'[ -~]{4,}',data_blob):
    text=m.group().decode('ascii','replace')
    for label,rule in data_rules.items():
        if rule.search(text):
            data_categories[label]['count']+=1
            if len(data_categories[label]['samples'])<40:
                data_categories[label]['samples'].append({'offset':data_sec['payload_offset']+m.start(),'text':text[:1000]})

# Explicit AI-tool token pass. Exact hits are reported with an offset/context;
# absence is more meaningful than style-based speculation.
ai_tokens=(b'claude',b'anthropic',b'openai',b'chatgpt',b'codex',b'copilot',b'cursor')
ai_token_hits=[]
lower=b.lower()
for token in ai_tokens:
    at=0
    while True:
        at=lower.find(token,at)
        if at<0: break
        ai_token_hits.append({'token':token.decode(),'offset':at,'context':b[max(0,at-80):at+len(token)+160].decode('ascii','replace')})
        at+=len(token)
glue=JS.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
glue_terms=['Emscripten','em-pthread','WebAssembly.Memory','wasmMemory','PThread','game.wasm','__main_argc_argv','wasm_httpfs_manifest_js','wgpu_start_worker']
glue_hits=[]
for term in glue_terms:
    at=glue.find(term)
    if at>=0: glue_hits.append({'term':term,'byte_offset':at,'line':glue.count('\n',0,at)+1,'context':glue[max(0,at-120):at+len(term)+180]})
out={'files':{'game.wasm':{'bytes':len(b),'sha256':hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest(),'magic':b[:4].hex(),'version_le_u32':int.from_bytes(b[4:8],'little')},'game.js':{'bytes':len(JS.read_bytes()),'sha256':hashlib.sha256(JS.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}},'sections':sections,'custom_sections':custom,'imports':imports,'exports':exports,'defined_memory_sections':memories,'targeted_printable_string_hits':hits,'priority_string_hits':priority_hits,'name_section_symbol_summary':name_summary,'data_section_summary':{'payload_offset':data_sec['payload_offset'],'payload_bytes':len(data_blob),'categories':data_categories},'ai_tool_token_hits':ai_token_hits,'game_js_hits':glue_hits,'counts':{'sections':len(sections),'imports':len(imports),'exports':len(exports),'custom_sections':len(custom),'targeted_string_hits':len(hits),'ai_tool_token_hits':len(ai_token_hits)}}
(ROOT/'snapshot/engine-evidence.json').write_text(json.dumps(out,indent=2)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
print(json.dumps({'sections':[(x['name'],x['payload_bytes']) for x in sections],'custom':custom,'imports':len(imports),'exports':len(exports),'hits':hits[:30]},indent=2))

FILE: homepage.html

<!doctype html>
<html>
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<title>game</title>
<style>
	html, body { margin: 0; height: 100%; background: #000; color: #ccc; font: 12px monospace; overflow: hidden; }
	#canvas { display: block; position: absolute; left: 0; top: 0; width: 100vw; height: 100vh; background: #000; }
	/* title screen: the game's loading screen (LOADINGSCREEN_STARTUP) on its 1280x720 stage, scaled to the window; see the script below */
	@font-face { font-family: 'Chalet'; src: url('/b/8b0b5899ed/title/chalet.woff') format('woff'); }
	#loading { position: fixed; inset: 0; background: #000; overflow: hidden; transition: opacity .8s; z-index: 5; }
	#loading.done { opacity: 0; pointer-events: none; }
	#ls-stage { position: absolute; left: 0; top: 0; width: 1280px; height: 720px; transform-origin: 0 0; overflow: hidden; font: 12px Chalet, sans-serif; color: #fff; }
	#ls-screens { position: absolute; inset: 0; }
	.ls-screen { position: absolute; inset: 0; }
	/* one camera per layer, so layers composite in display-list order like Scaleform (no depth test): 640 / tan(55 deg / 2) = 1229, Scaleform's default 3D camera
	   at the stage centre (moved against the screen's slide, see the script) */
	.ls-cam { position: absolute; inset: 0; perspective: 1229px; perspective-origin: 640px 360px; }
	.ls-layer { position: absolute; left: 0; top: 0; transform-origin: 0 0; background-size: 100% 100%; }
	#ls-logo { position: absolute; left: 95px; top: 492.5px; width: 186px; height: 186px; }
	#ls-bars { position: absolute; right: 96px; top: 642px; display: flex; gap: 3px; }

	#modes { gap: 8px; }
	#modes button { display: flex; align-items: center; height: 24px; padding: 0; border: 0; background: none; color: #fff; font: 12px Chalet, sans-serif; cursor: pointer; }
	#modes button:disabled { cursor: default; }
	#modes button[hidden] { display: none; }
	#modes.ask button:not(:disabled) .ls-key { animation: ls-ask 1.2s ease-in-out infinite; }
	@keyframes ls-ask { 50% { opacity: .35; } }

	.ls-spin { flex: none; width: 22px; height: 22px; margin-left: 2px; background: url('/b/8b0b5899ed/title/spinner.png') center / 24px 24px no-repeat; animation: ls-spin 2s steps(60) infinite; }
	@keyframes ls-spin { from { transform: rotate(0deg) scaleX(-1); } to { transform: rotate(-360deg) scaleX(-1); } }
	#label { text-shadow: .85px .85px 1px rgba(0, 0, 0, .45); }

	#ls-log { position: absolute; left: 8px; right: 8px; bottom: 6px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; font: 10px/1.35 Consolas, monospace; color: rgba(190, 190, 190, .75); text-shadow: 0 0 2px #000, 1px 1px 1px #000; white-space: pre; pointer-events: none; }
	#console { max-width: 100%; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }
	#data { color: #6a9; }
	#bar { position: absolute; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; height: 3px; background: rgba(255, 255, 255, .08); }
	#fill { height: 100%; width: 0; background: #7cc576; }

	#fps { position: fixed; left: 8px; top: 6px; display: none; color: #0f0; font: bold 18px/1.2 Consolas, monospace; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; text-shadow: 1px 1px 2px #000, 0 0 3px #000; pointer-events: none; z-index: 10; }
</style>

<body>
<canvas id="canvas" width="800" height="600" tabindex="-1"></canvas>
<div id="hud"></div>
<div id="loading"><div id="ls-stage"><div id="ls-screens"></div><img id="ls-logo" src="/b/8b0b5899ed/title/logo.png" alt=""><div id="ls-bars"><div id="modes" class="ls-bar"><button data-act="back"></button><button data-act="map5"></button><button data-act="map6"></button><button data-act="sandbox"></button><button data-act="story"></button></div><div class="ls-bar"><span id="label">Starting...</span><span id="pct">0%</span><i class="ls-spin"></i></div></div></div><div id="ls-log"><div id="data"></div><div id="console"></div></div><div id="bar"><div id="fill"></div></div></div>
<div id="fps"></div>
<script type="module">
// Page of the browser build. The engine runs in a dedicated worker (loader.js: its "main thread", where the static constructors and
// main() run and which may block); the page only owns the canvas, which is transferred to that worker and on to the GPU worker.
// Query string: ?args=-flag1,-flag2=value (added to the engine command line), ?shot=N (screenshot of every N-th present to the dev server).
const q = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
// URL prefix of everything this page loads (loader, workers, engine, shaders, title art): empty on the dev server; on the PHP host (phase3/host/index.php) it puts the page's own
// text `/b/8b0b5899ed` through str_replace to `/b/<build id>`, so those files are cached for ever under URLs that change when the build does.
const BASE = '/b/8b0b5899ed'.charAt(0) === '_' ? '' : '/b/8b0b5899ed';
// ?log=1: the page, the engine threads and the workers send their log lines to the dev server (browser.log, browser_<ip>.log); off by default: the player's upload
// bandwidth and this thread's time. The loading screen's own log line and the browser console do not depend on it.
const remoteLog = q.get('log') === '1';
const postLog = (text) => { if (remoteLog) fetch('/log', { method: 'POST', body: text }).catch(() => {}); };
// Crash reports from the engine threads (prejs.js __crash, loader.js onAbort/onExit): shown in the bottom-left line with a hint when it looks like memory, and posted to the host's log
// only with ?log=1 (at most 3 per page, ~10 KB each; they were always posted until 2026-10-06, when 10,000 visitors a day made 5,500 posts and a log file per visitor). A normal exit does not report.
{
	let posted = 0;
	new BroadcastChannel('game-crash').onmessage = (ev) => {
		const m = ev.data;
		const memory = /memory|OOM|Aborted\(|RangeError|grow/i.test(m.text);
		const dev = ' deviceMemory ' + (navigator.deviceMemory || '?') + ' GB, cores ' + navigator.hardwareConcurrency;
		if (remoteLog && posted++ < 3) fetch('/log', { method: 'POST', body: '[page] CRASH (' + m.kind + ', ' + m.thread + ' thread, wasm heap ' + m.heap + ' MB,' + dev + ', ' + location.search + '): ' + m.text + '\n' + m.tail.join('\n') + '\n' }).catch(() => {});
		const el = document.getElementById('hud');
		if (el) { el.textContent = 'The game stopped (' + m.kind + '): ' + m.text.split('\n')[0].slice(0, 200) + (memory ? ' - this looks like a lack of memory: close other tabs and reload.' : ' - reload the page.'); el.style.color = '#f96'; }
	};
}
// ---- title screen: the game's own loading screen, LOADINGSCREEN_STARTUP (scaleform_frontend.rpf), redone in HTML ----------------------------------------
// Ported from its ActionScript (com.rockstargames.gtav.loadingScreens.LOADINGSCREEN_STARTUP, decompiled with JPEXS FFDec) as it plays while the game loads
// (CTX_GAMELOAD). On the 1280x720 stage (scaled to the window, black outside 16:9): the built-in beach screen and the 17 loadingscreen<N> art screens in one of
// the movie's six random orders; each is a background panel tilted in 3D (Scaleform's camera: 55 degree field of view around the stage centre, pivot at the
// layer's top-left; Scaleform's +Z points away from the viewer, CSS's towards it, hence the negated angles) and foreground cut-outs. Every 14 s the next screen
// slides in (0.85 s, cubic in-out, from alternating sides) and the old one out; a screen's layers tween linearly to their end values over 42 s. The GTA V logo
// sits bottom left and the instructional button bar bottom right at the boot-time safe zone (7.5 %, CHudTools::GetFakeLoadingScreenSafeZone).
// Art, logo, spinner and the font ($Font2 = Chalet London 1960) are the game's (phase3/make_title.py, served at /b/8b0b5899ed/title/).
let stopTitle = () => {};
{
	const stage = document.getElementById('ls-stage'), holder = document.getElementById('ls-screens');
	const fit = () => {
		const s = Math.min(innerWidth / 1280, innerHeight / 720);
		stage.style.transform = 'translate(' + (innerWidth - 1280 * s) / 2 + 'px,' + (innerHeight - 720 * s) / 2 + 'px) scale(' + s + ')';
	};
	fit();
	addEventListener('resize', fit);
	const L = (tex, w, h, x, y, yr, to) => ({ tex, w, h, x, y, yr, to });
	const BG = (n, yr, to, x = 98) => L('ls' + n + '_background', 1024, 512, x, 95, yr, to);
	const FG = (n, name, w, h, x, y, toX, yr = 0) => L('ls' + n + '_' + name, w, h, x, y, yr, { x: toX });
	const SCREENS = [		// the movie's loadingScreens array: setup (size, position, _yrotation) and tweenTo of every layer, bottom to top
		[L('beach_bg', 1024, 512, 98, 95, 20, { yr: -6, xr: 5 }), L('beach_fg', 343, 629, 480, 95, 0, { x: 720 })],
		null,		// loadingscreen_builtin1 (Frisk): its symbols are not in this build's movie, and no order uses it
		[BG(0, 7, { yr: -7 }), FG(0, 'foreground', 299, 599, 360, 140, 560, 7)],
		[BG(1, 6, { yr: -12 }), FG(1, 'foreground', 503, 206, 300, 465, 480), FG(1, 'foreground_franklin', 368, 588, 440, 140, 745)],
		[BG(2, 7, { yr: -5, xr: 5 }), FG(2, 'foreground', 311, 558, 745, 200, 450), FG(2, 'foreground_chop', 320, 512, 640, 260, 310)],
		[BG(3, 12, { yr: -4 }, 110), FG(3, 'foreground', 412, 609, 440, 120, 730)],		// its foreground_car is an "isInFla" layer missing from the movie: the game shows no car
		[BG(4, 7, { yr: -4, xr: 5 }), FG(4, 'foreground', 251, 643, 400, 85, 250)],
		[BG(5, 7, { yr: -6 }), FG(5, 'foreground', 389, 640, 450, 95, 700)],
		[BG(6, 7, { yr: 0, xr: 5 }), FG(6, 'foreground', 367, 603, 410, 115, 800)],
		[BG(7, 6, { yr: -6, xr: 5 }), FG(7, 'foreground', 311, 625, 860, 95, 520)],
		[BG(8, 7, { yr: -5, xr: 5 }), FG(8, 'foreground', 492, 497, 400, 190, 660)],
		[BG(9, 7, { yr: -3, xr: 5 }), FG(9, 'foreground', 331, 675, 220, 50, 140)],
		[BG(10, 6, { yr: -5, xr: 5 }), FG(10, 'foreground', 352, 682, 545, 65, 485)],
		[BG(11, 7, { yr: -3, xr: 5 }), FG(11, 'foreground', 344, 669, 309, 51, 424)],
		[BG(12, 6, { yr: -4 }), FG(12, 'foreground_michael', 462, 677, 345, 42, 220)],
		[BG(13, 5, { yr: -6, xr: 5 }), FG(13, 'foreground', 399, 681, 430, 39, 250)],
		[BG(14, 6, { yr: -2, xr: 5 }), FG(14, 'foreground', 392, 631, 650, 95, 850)],
		[BG(15, 6, { yr: -3, xr: 5 }), FG(15, 'foreground', 385, 642, 404, 78, 261)],
		[BG(16, 6, { yr: -3, xr: 5 }), FG(16, 'foreground', 300, 600, 300, 150, 400)],
	];
	const ORDERS = [[0, 16, 18, 3, 12, 2, 17, 10, 9, 8, 15, 6, 11, 14, 4, 7, 13], [0, 4, 6, 17, 11, 16, 18, 7, 9, 15, 3, 14, 10, 13, 2, 8, 12],
		[0, 7, 16, 14, 13, 3, 10, 2, 15, 18, 8, 9, 4, 17, 12, 6, 11], [0, 8, 18, 9, 13, 4, 11, 14, 7, 17, 6, 12, 3, 16, 2, 15, 10],
		[0, 11, 12, 7, 18, 14, 9, 13, 4, 2, 15, 11, 8, 16, 6, 3, 17], [0, 10, 16, 2, 13, 17, 8, 9, 18, 3, 12, 6, 7, 4, 14, 15, 10]];
	const order = ORDERS[Math.floor(Math.random() * ORDERS.length)];
	const cubicInOut = (t) => (t < 0.5 ? 4 * t * t * t : 1 - Math.pow(2 - 2 * t, 3) / 2);		// TweenStar Cubic.easeInOut
	// the screen's horizontal position; its layers' cameras stay at the stage centre (Scaleform's camera does not move with the clip)
	const slide = (s, x) => {
		s.el.style.transform = 'translateX(' + x + 'px)';
		for (const c of s.cams) c.style.perspectiveOrigin = (640 - x) + 'px 360px';
	};
	const place = (l, x, yr, xr) => { l.el.style.transform = 'translate3d(' + x + 'px,' + l.y + 'px,0) rotateY(' + -yr + 'deg) rotateX(' + -xr + 'deg)'; };
	// a screen's images, decoded before it may show (the movie waits for its texture dictionary the same way)
	const prepare = (i) => {
		const layers = SCREENS[order[i]].map((d) => ({ ...d, img: Object.assign(new Image(), { src: '/b/8b0b5899ed/title/art/' + d.tex + '.webp' }) }));
		return Promise.all(layers.map((l) => l.img.decode().catch(() => {}))).then(() => layers);
	};
	let active = null, leaving = null, index = 0, alt = -1, timer = 0, raf = 0, stopped = false;
	const show = (defs) => {
		const now = performance.now();
		alt = alt <= 0 ? 1 : -1;		// updateScreenIndex: the side alternates
		const el = document.createElement('div');
		el.className = 'ls-screen';
		const cams = [];
		const s = { el, slideFrom: alt * 1280, start: now, settled: 0, cams, layers: defs.map((d) => {
			const cam = document.createElement('div'), e = document.createElement('div');
			cam.className = 'ls-cam';
			e.className = 'ls-layer';
			e.style.width = d.w + 'px'; e.style.height = d.h + 'px';
			e.style.backgroundImage = 'url(' + d.img.src + ')';
			cam.appendChild(e);
			el.appendChild(cam);
			cams.push(cam);
			return { el: e, y: d.y, x0: d.x, yr0: d.yr, xr0: 0, x1: d.to.x ?? d.x, yr1: d.to.yr ?? d.yr, xr1: d.to.xr ?? 0 };
		}) };
		for (const l of s.layers) place(l, l.x0, l.yr0, l.xr0);
		slide(s, s.slideFrom);
		holder.appendChild(el);		// above the screen that leaves
		if (leaving) leaving.el.remove();
		leaving = active;
		if (leaving) { leaving.slideFrom = 0; leaving.slideTo = -alt * 1280; leaving.start = now; }
		active = s;
		s.slideTo = 0;
	};
	const tick = (now) => {
		for (const s of [leaving, active]) {
			if (!s) continue;
			const p = Math.min(1, (now - s.start) / 850);
			if (p < 1 || !s.slid) { slide(s, s.slideFrom + (s.slideTo - s.slideFrom) * cubicInOut(p)); s.slid = p === 1; }
			if (s === active && p === 1 && !s.settled) s.settled = now;		// loadscreenIsSettled: the layers start their 42 s tweens
			if (s.settled) {
				const q = Math.min(1, (now - s.settled) / 42000);
				for (const l of s.layers) place(l, l.x0 + (l.x1 - l.x0) * q, l.yr0 + (l.yr1 - l.yr0) * q, l.xr0 + (l.xr1 - l.xr0) * q);
			}
			if (s === leaving && p === 1) { s.el.remove(); leaving = null; }
		}
		if (!stopped) raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
	};
	let next = prepare(0);
	const cycle = () => next.then((defs) => {
		if (stopped) return;
		show(defs);
		index = (index + 1) % order.length;
		next = prepare(index);
		timer = setTimeout(cycle, 14000);		// timerDuration
	});
	timer = setTimeout(cycle, 500);
	raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
	stopTitle = () => { stopped = true; clearTimeout(timer); cancelAnimationFrame(raf); };
}
// ---- start choice: Story Mode / Sandbox Mode, and Sandbox Mode's map, in the title screen's button bar ---------------------------------------------------------
// The game's own loading-time choice (CLoadingScreens::RefreshButtonLayout: "Loading Story Mode" with a spinner, "Online" as a button) with Sandbox Mode in the
// Online slot. Buttons are laid out like updateButtonLayout: the spinner item rightmost; on PC the icons are key caps (a click works too). Enter = Story Mode;
// Space = Sandbox Mode, which asks for the map: 5 = the GTA V map, 6 = "GTA VI Map" (the env_test level), Backspace = back. The choice goes into the engine's
// input block (WasmInputBlock.startMode, wasm/platform/input/browser_input_wasm.cpp): 1 = Story Mode (the release startup: a new game from the Prologue),
// 2 = Sandbox Mode on the GTA V map (free roam at a random spot with every weapon), 3 = Sandbox Mode on env_test. It can change until the engine takes it
// (where it reads -level, about 62 % into the load; it waits there if nothing was chosen, and the page then pulses the key caps); after that only the chosen
// item stays. ?mode=story|sandbox chooses up front (with &map=env_test for Sandbox Mode on env_test).
let startMode = q.get('mode') === 'story' ? 1 : q.get('mode') === 'sandbox' ? (q.get('map') === 'env_test' ? 3 : 2) : 0;
let startModeWord = null, startCommitted = false, pickingMap = false, waitingChoice = false;		// waitingChoice: the engine waits for the choice
const ACTS = {		// button: text, key cap, key codes
	back: ['Back', 'Backspace', ['Backspace']],
	map5: ['GTA V Map', '5', ['Digit5', 'Numpad5']],
	map6: ['GTA VI Map', '6', ['Digit6', 'Numpad6']],
	sandbox: ['Sandbox Mode', 'Space', ['Space']],
	story: ['Story Mode', 'Enter', ['Enter', 'NumpadEnter']],
};
const LOADING = { 1: 'Loading Story Mode', 2: 'Loading Sandbox Mode: GTA V Map', 3: 'Loading Sandbox Mode: GTA VI Map' };
const modesEl = document.getElementById('modes'), modeButtons = [...modesEl.querySelectorAll('button')];
const isMode = (act) => act === 'story' || act === 'sandbox';
const showModes = () => {
	for (const b of modeButtons) {
		const act = b.dataset.act, loading = !pickingMap && (act === 'story' ? startMode === 1 : act === 'sandbox' && startMode >= 2);
		const text = document.createElement('span'), icon = document.createElement(loading ? 'i' : 'span');
		text.textContent = loading ? LOADING[startMode] : ACTS[act][0];
		icon.className = loading ? 'ls-spin' : 'ls-key';
		icon.textContent = loading ? '' : ACTS[act][1];
		b.replaceChildren(text, icon);
		b.style.order = loading ? 1 : 0;
		b.disabled = startCommitted;
		b.hidden = startCommitted ? !loading : pickingMap === isMode(act);		// the modes, or the map choice with Back
		b.classList.toggle('chosen', loading);
	}
	if (startMode) modesEl.classList.remove('ask');
	if (waitingChoice && !startMode) document.getElementById('label').textContent = pickingMap ? 'Choose a map to continue' : 'Choose Story Mode or Sandbox Mode to continue';
};
const choose = (m) => {
	pickingMap = false;
	if (m !== startMode) {
		startMode = m;
		if (startModeWord) Atomics.store(startModeWord, 0, m);
		postLog('[page] start mode: ' + ({ 1: 'story', 2: 'sandbox', 3: 'sandbox on env_test' })[m]);
	}
	showModes();
};
const act = (a) => {
	if (startCommitted) return;
	if (a === 'story') choose(1);
	else if (a === 'map5') choose(2);
	else if (a === 'map6') choose(3);
	else { pickingMap = a === 'sandbox'; showModes(); }		// Sandbox Mode (or its loading item: change the map) asks for the map; Back closes that
};
modesEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => { const b = e.target.closest('button'); if (b && !b.hidden) act(b.dataset.act); });
addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
	if (startCommitted || e.ctrlKey || e.altKey || e.metaKey || !document.getElementById('loading')) return;
	const b = modeButtons.find((x) => !x.hidden && ACTS[x.dataset.act][2].includes(e.code));
	if (!b) return;
	act(b.dataset.act);
	e.preventDefault(); e.stopImmediatePropagation();
}, { capture: true });
showModes();
// ---- loading progress: the engine (prejs.js, loader.js) and the GPU worker publish {pct, label} on a BroadcastChannel ------------------
{
	const loading = document.getElementById('loading'), fill = document.getElementById('fill'), label = document.getElementById('label'), pct = document.getElementById('pct');
	let target = 0, shown = 0, ready = false, dirty = false, loadingGone = false;
	let dataBytes = 0, worldBase = -1;
	const samples = [];
	let lastLine = '';
	const consoleEl = document.getElementById('console');
	const bc = new BroadcastChannel('game-progress');
	let lastActivity = performance.now(), stallLogged = 0, lastLabel = '';
	bc.onmessage = (ev) => {
		const m = ev.data;
		lastActivity = performance.now();
		if (m.dl) {		// bytes of game data fetched by an engine thread since its last report
			dataBytes += m.dl;
			samples.push([performance.now(), m.dl]);
			return;
		}
		if (m.lines) {
			if (loadingGone || !m.lines.length) return;		// the log line above the progress bar is gone: no DOM work for it during play
			lastLine = m.lines[m.lines.length - 1];
			dirty = true;
			return;
		}
		if (m.gpuLost) { hud.textContent = 'The graphics device was lost (' + m.gpuLost + '): the screen will stay black. Reload the page.'; hud.style.color = '#f66'; return; }
		if (m.error) { label.textContent = m.error; label.style.color = '#e66'; return; }
		if (m.pct > target) { target = m.pct; if (m.label) { lastLabel = m.label; if (!waitingChoice) label.textContent = m.label; } if (m.pct >= 72 && worldBase < 0) worldBase = dataBytes; }
		if (m.choice && !startMode) { waitingChoice = true; modesEl.classList.add('ask'); showModes(); }		// the engine waits for the choice: showModes asks for it in the label
		if (m.commit) { startCommitted = true; pickingMap = false; if (m.mode) startMode = m.mode; showModes(); }		// the engine took the choice
		if (m.compiling !== undefined && !waitingChoice) { label.textContent = lastLabel = 'Preparing shaders'; target = Math.max(target, 99); }		// the world is up; the GPU worker holds it until its pipelines are compiled
		if (m.world) { ready = true; hud.textContent = 'click the game to capture the mouse (Esc releases it, Shift+P switches keyboard mode, = shows FPS)'; setTimeout(() => { hud.textContent = ''; }, 15000); }
	};
	// Between reports the bar creeps forward (at most 6 points past the last report) so a long stage does not look frozen.
	const dataEl = document.getElementById('data');
	const WORLD_BYTES = 1.2e9;		// game data the world needs after the scripts start (measured: about 1.2-1.4 GB the first time)
	// Stall watchdog: nothing (no log line, no data, no progress) from any engine thread for 30 s while loading. A tab that ran out of memory dies ("Aw, Snap!") and cannot say so,
	// but one that is thrashing or deadlocked can: show it, and leave a timestamped line in the server log for diagnosis.
	setInterval(() => {
		if (ready) return;
		if (waitingChoice) { if (!startMode) { lastActivity = performance.now(); return; } waitingChoice = false; label.textContent = lastLabel; }
		const quiet = (performance.now() - lastActivity) / 1000;
		if (quiet > 30) {
			label.textContent = 'No progress for ' + (quiet | 0) + ' s' + (lastLabel ? ' (last step: ' + lastLabel + ')' : '') + ': the device may be out of memory. Close other tabs, or reload.';
			label.style.color = '#e96';
			if (quiet - stallLogged > 30) { stallLogged = quiet; postLog('[page] STALL: no progress for ' + (quiet | 0) + ' s, last step "' + lastLabel + '", shown ' + shown.toFixed(0) + '%'); }
		} else if (stallLogged) { stallLogged = 0; label.style.color = ''; }
	}, 2000);
	const progressTimer = setInterval(() => {
		const now = performance.now();
		while (samples.length && now - samples[0][0] > 3000) samples.shift();
		const speed = samples.reduce((a, x) => a + x[1], 0) / 3;
		if (dataBytes) dataEl.textContent = 'Game data: ' + (dataBytes / 1048576 | 0) + ' MB' + (speed > 1e4 ? ' (' + (speed / 1048576).toFixed(1) + ' MB/s)' : '');
		// from the scripts stage on, progress also follows the data that arrives (72 -> 98 %)
		if (worldBase >= 0 && !ready) target = Math.max(target, 72 + 26 * Math.min(1, (dataBytes - worldBase) / WORLD_BYTES));
		const cap = ready ? 100 : Math.min(99, target + 6);
		const goal = ready ? 100 : (shown < target ? target : cap);
		shown += (goal - shown) * (goal === target || ready ? 0.12 : 0.004);
		if (dirty) {		// the newest line only
			dirty = false;
			consoleEl.textContent = lastLine;
		}
		fill.style.width = shown.toFixed(1) + '%';
		pct.textContent = Math.round(shown) + '%';
		if (ready && shown > 99.5) { loading.classList.add('done'); setTimeout(() => { loading.remove(); stopTitle(); }, 1000); loadingGone = true; clearInterval(progressTimer); }		// once: the page does no loading-screen work during play
	}, 50);
}
const hud = document.getElementById('hud');
// FPS counter, top left: off by default, the = key toggles it. The GPU worker counts presented frames and posts the rate twice a second on 'game-fps'. The key does not reach
// the game (capture phase on window, before the input handlers; this build has debug key bindings).
{
	const el = document.getElementById('fps');
	let on = false;
	new BroadcastChannel('game-fps').onmessage = (ev) => { if (on) el.textContent = Math.round(ev.data.fps) + ' FPS'; };
	const isToggle = (e) => e.code === 'Equal' && !e.ctrlKey && !e.altKey && !e.metaKey;
	addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
		if (!isToggle(e)) return;
		e.stopImmediatePropagation(); e.preventDefault();
		if (e.repeat) return;
		on = !on;
		el.style.display = on ? 'block' : 'none';
		if (on) el.textContent = '-- FPS';
	}, { capture: true });
	addEventListener('keyup', (e) => { if (isToggle(e)) { e.stopImmediatePropagation(); e.preventDefault(); } }, { capture: true });
}
const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas');
// With ?log=1, log lines of the engine threads and the GPU worker arrive here (prejs.js / wgpu_worker.js post them on 'game-log' without blocking) and go to the dev server
// in order, one POST at a time with whatever queued up meanwhile.
if (remoteLog) {
	const queue = [];
	let sending = false;
	const pump = async () => {
		if (sending || !queue.length) return;
		sending = true;
		const body = queue.splice(0).join('\n');
		try { await fetch('/log', { method: 'POST', body, keepalive: body.length < 60000 }); } catch (e) { /* server gone */ }
		sending = false;
		pump();
	};
	new BroadcastChannel('game-log').onmessage = (ev) => { queue.push(ev.data); if (queue.length > 20000) queue.splice(0, queue.length - 20000); pump(); };
}
// Release the io worker's exclusive storage handles as soon as this page goes away (a quick reload would otherwise find them locked and run without the store).
addEventListener('pagehide', () => { try { new BroadcastChannel('game-io').postMessage('close'); } catch (e) {} });
// Earlier versions cached the game data with a service worker (sw.js) and the Cache API: remove both, the io worker keeps its own store (io_worker.js).
async function dropOldCache() {
	try {
		if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) for (const r of await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistrations()) await r.unregister();
		if (window.caches) for (const k of await caches.keys()) if (k.startsWith('gamedata')) await caches.delete(k);
	} catch (e) { console.warn('could not remove the old cache: ' + e); }
}
let lowMemory = false;		// set below (module scope: the ?mem readout further down also needs it)
let startSize = [1280, 720], wantSize = null;		// set below (module scope: installInput uses them)
if (!crossOriginIsolated) { document.getElementById('label').textContent = 'not cross-origin isolated: open the page over https (or localhost); the server sends COOP/COEP'; document.getElementById('label').style.color = '#e66'; }
else {
	const offscreen = canvas.transferControlToOffscreen();
	await dropOldCache();
	// Low-memory profile (?low=1 forces it, ?low=0 forbids it; default: navigator.deviceMemory <= 4, which browsers report as 4 for 4 GB and less):
	// every engine thread is a Web Worker of ~20 MB and the engine sizes its thread count from the core count, so pretend to have 2 cores (54 threads
	// instead of 80-116); quarter the texture memory (texture quality 0 = top mip skipped on the GPU), no network stack, and the minimum ped/vehicle
	// variety, city density, LOD and quality settings: measured in a Node run at the world, GPU memory 1.16 GB -> 0.5 GB and streamed assets 1.38 GB -> 0.95 GB.
	// Shadows off (-shadowQuality=-1, engine level 0 below the menu's lowest; SettingsManager.cpp, wasm): the cascade shadow pass was a third of all draws (Node, 1366x600:
	// 600-900 of 1,300-2,900 per frame), and a 4-core Chromebook spent ~110 us per draw. ?shadows=1 keeps them at the lowest level.
	const lowParam = q.get('low');
	lowMemory = lowParam === '1' || (lowParam !== '0' && navigator.deviceMemory && navigator.deviceMemory <= 4);
	const LOW_ARGS = ['-textureQuality=0', '-pedVariety=0', '-vehicleVariety=0', '-shadowQuality=' + (q.get('shadows') === '1' ? 0 : -1), '-reflectionQuality=0', '-particleQuality=0',
		'-grassQuality=0', '-cityDensity=0', '-lodScale=0', '-pedLodBias=0', '-vehicleLodBias=0'];
	const cores = +(q.get('cores') || 0) || (lowMemory ? 2 : navigator.hardwareConcurrency);
	if (lowMemory) hud.textContent = 'Low-memory device (' + (navigator.deviceMemory || '?') + ' GB reported): close other tabs. If the tab shows "Aw, Snap!" or SIGILL it ran out of memory.';
postLog('[page env] ' + JSON.stringify({ deviceMemory: navigator.deviceMemory, cores: navigator.hardwareConcurrency, usingCores: cores, low: lowMemory, isolated: crossOriginIsolated, ua: navigator.userAgent.slice(0, 120), screen: screen.width + 'x' + screen.height + '@' + devicePixelRatio }));
// Back buffer = the browser window in device pixels (native resolution), following window resizes live (see installInput). ?scale=0.75 renders at 75 % (faster), ?fixed=1 keeps the
// start-up size whatever the window does, ?res=1920x1080 forces a size. Even numbers, at most 3840x2160 (2560x1440 in the low-memory profile: screen-sized render targets dominate GPU memory).
wantSize = () => {
	const forced = /^(\d+)x(\d+)$/.exec(q.get('res') || '');
	if (forced) return [+forced[1], +forced[2]];
	const sc = Math.max(0.25, Math.min(2, +(q.get('scale') || 1)));
	let w = innerWidth * devicePixelRatio * sc, h = innerHeight * devicePixelRatio * sc;
	const cap = lowMemory ? [2560, 1440] : [3840, 2160], k = Math.min(1, cap[0] / w, cap[1] / h);
	w *= k; h *= k;
	return [Math.max(320, Math.round(w / 2) * 2), Math.max(240, Math.round(h / 2) * 2)];
};
startSize = wantSize();
// Script and replay channels: ~87 % of all log lines in play (the scripts print leaderboard and status lines every few frames). Below warning they are off: formatting, the
// log transport and the dev server's log file cost the main thread for nothing. ?verbose=1 keeps everything.
const LOG_ARGS = q.get('verbose') ? [] : ['-Script_all=warning', '-replay_all=warning'];
// -fastscriptcore: the script interpreter's production core (scrThread::Run static). Without it this build runs the debugging core, which looks up two breakpoint tables
// for every script instruction (scrThread::Run was ~8 % of the main thread).
const ENGINE_ARGS = ['-fastscriptcore'];
// Chrome on Windows rounds every wait with a timeout (Atomics.wait: the engine's sleeps, its 1 ms event waits, the frame limiter, the yields of its spin loops) up to the OS
// timer tick, 15.6 ms, unless the renderer process has a timer below 16 ms pending, which switches the process to a fine tick (measured: a 1 ms wait takes 15.5 ms; with
// this timer 2.1 ms, a 4 ms wait 5.0 ms). The game has no such timer, so every short sleep cost a whole tick: the limiter held a 60 fps cap to 37 fps and the visibility
// wait added 15 ms to a frame. An empty callback; nothing else on this thread runs while the game plays.
setInterval(() => {}, 1);
const worker = new Worker(BASE + '/loader.js');
	worker.onmessage = (ev) => {
		if (ev.data.hud) hud.textContent = ev.data.hud;
		if (ev.data.input) installInput(ev.data.input.memory, ev.data.input.block);
		if (ev.data.audio) { audioPending = ev.data.audio; tryStartAudio(); }
	};
	worker.onerror = (e) => { hud.textContent = 'worker error: ' + e.message; };
	worker.postMessage({
		canvas: offscreen,
		base: BASE,
		args: ['-rootdir=/game/', ...(q.get('nogpu') ? [] : ['-wgpu']), '-nodisplaycalibration', '-noSocialClub', '-pc:nosocialclub', ...(q.get('newgame') === '1' ? ['-noautoload'] : []), '-nonetwork', '-output', '-width=' + startSize[0], '-height=' + startSize[1], '-forceResolution', ...LOG_ARGS, ...ENGINE_ARGS, '-frameLimit=' + (q.get('fps') === '30' ? 2 : q.get('fps') === '0' ? 0 : 1),		// the game's own limiter: VSync 2 (its default) = 30 fps; 1 = 60 fps; ?fps=0 uncapped (diagnostics). Story Mode continues from the newest savegame (the engine skips that for Sandbox Mode); ?newgame=1 (-noautoload) starts it from the Prologue instead
		 ...(cores <= 2 ? ['-numCores=' + cores] : []), ...(lowMemory ? LOW_ARGS : []), ...(q.get('args') || '').split(',').filter(Boolean)],
		shotEvery: +(q.get('shot') || 0),
		shotRt: q.get('shotrt') === '1',		// ?shotrt=1 (with ?shot=N): every render target of one frame after the world is shown to the dev server's shots/ (wgpu_worker.js)
		noHints: q.get('nohints') === '1',		// ?nohints=1: do not fetch the streamer's queued reads ahead (io_worker.js drainHints; diagnostics)
		noPack: q.get('nopack') === '1',		// ?nopack=1: fetch shaders one by one, not from the shader packs (wgpu_worker.js; diagnostics)
		gpuLimits: q.get('limits') || '',		// ?limits=maxSampledTexturesPerShaderStage:16,... : request a smaller GPU device than the adapter offers (reproduces a weaker device's limits; wgpu_worker.js)
		keepThreads: (q.get('keep') || '').split(',').reduce((m, g) => m | ({ replay: 1, net: 2, bink: 4, idle: 8 }[g] || 0), 0),		// ?keep=replay,net,bink,idle: start those thread groups (diagnostics)
		cores,
		noStore: !!(q.get('nocache') || q.get('nosw')),
		fullConsole: !!q.get('console'),
		remoteLog,		// ?log=1 (above): loader.js passes it on to the engine threads and the GPU and io workers
		trace: !!q.get('trace'),		// ?trace=1: log every engine file request (io_worker.js)
		slots: +(q.get('slots') || 0),		// ?slots=N: frames in flight of the GPU worker (diagnostics)
		syncPipelines: !!q.get('syncpipelines'),		// ?syncpipelines=1: the GPU worker compiles pipelines synchronously (no draws skipped while one compiles)
		record: !!q.get('record'),		// ?record=1: log the boot read set (io_worker.js), for record_bootset.py
		lowMemory,
	}, [offscreen]);
}

// ?mem=1 (diagnostics, needs ?log=1): performance.measureUserAgentSpecificMemory() every 15 s (needs cross-origin isolation), posted to the dev server's log.
if (remoteLog && (q.get('mem') || lowMemory) && performance.measureUserAgentSpecificMemory) {		// with ?log=1 on by default in the low-memory profile: the last reading before a crash/stall is in the log
	const report = async () => {
		try {
			const r = await performance.measureUserAgentSpecificMemory();
			const rows = r.breakdown.filter((b) => b.bytes > 1e6).map((b) => (b.bytes / 1048576 | 0) + ' MB ' + (b.types || []).join('+') + ' ' + (b.attribution || []).map((a) => (a.url || '').split('/').pop() + '/' + (a.scope || '')).join(','));
			postLog('[page mem] total ' + (r.bytes / 1048576 | 0) + ' MB; ' + rows.join(' | '));
		} catch (e) { postLog('[page mem] failed: ' + e); }
		setTimeout(report, 15000);
	};
	setTimeout(report, 15000);
}

// ---- audio: the engine mixer writes into a ring in shared memory (platform/audio/browser_audio_wasm.cpp); an AudioWorklet plays it -------------------
// Browsers only allow audio after a user gesture: the context is created on the first click/key press.
let audioPending = null, audioGesture = false, audioStarted = false;
function tryStartAudio() {
	if (!audioPending || !audioGesture || audioStarted) return;
	audioStarted = true;
	(async () => {
		try {
			const ctx = new AudioContext({ sampleRate: 48000, latencyHint: 'interactive' });
			await ctx.audioWorklet.addModule(BASE + '/audio-worklet.js');
			const node = new AudioWorkletNode(ctx, 'game-audio', { numberOfInputs: 0, numberOfOutputs: 1, outputChannelCount: [2], processorOptions: audioPending });
			node.connect(ctx.destination);
			await ctx.resume();
			addEventListener('pointerdown', () => ctx.resume());
			addEventListener('keydown', () => ctx.resume());
			if (q.get('mem')) {		// diagnostics: ring state every 5 s
				const hdr = new Int32Array(audioPending.memory.buffer, audioPending.ring, 8);
				setInterval(() => postLog('[page audio] state ' + ctx.state + ', frames written ' + (hdr[0] >>> 0) + ', read ' + (hdr[1] >>> 0) + ', fill ' + (((hdr[0] - hdr[1]) >>> 0)) + ', worklet calls ' + (hdr[3] >>> 0) + ', peak ' + (Atomics.exchange(hdr, 4, 0) / 1e6).toFixed(3)), 5000);
			}
			postLog('[page] audio started: ' + ctx.sampleRate + ' Hz, base latency ' + ctx.baseLatency);
		} catch (e) { audioStarted = false; postLog('[page] audio failed: ' + e); }
	})();
}
for (const type of ['pointerdown', 'keydown']) addEventListener(type, () => { audioGesture = true; tryStartAudio(); }, { capture: true });

// ---- input: DOM events -> the engine's input block in shared memory (platform/input/browser_input_wasm.cpp) ------------------------
// Layout of WasmInputBlock: 256 key bytes, then int32 words: mouseX, mouseY, dX, dY, dZ, buttons, focus, locked, textHead, textTail, text[64] (uint16).
const VK = {
	Backspace: 0x08, Tab: 0x09, Enter: 0x0D, NumpadEnter: 0xFD, Pause: 0x13, CapsLock: 0x14, Escape: 0x1B, Space: 0x20, PageUp: 0x21, PageDown: 0x22,
	End: 0x23, Home: 0x24, ArrowLeft: 0x25, ArrowUp: 0x26, ArrowRight: 0x27, ArrowDown: 0x28, PrintScreen: 0x2C, Insert: 0x2D, Delete: 0x2E,
	MetaLeft: 0x5B, MetaRight: 0x5C, ContextMenu: 0x5D, NumpadMultiply: 0x6A, NumpadAdd: 0x6B, NumpadSubtract: 0x6D, NumpadDecimal: 0x6E, NumpadDivide: 0x6F,
	NumLock: 0x90, ScrollLock: 0x91, ShiftLeft: 0xA0, ShiftRight: 0xA1, ControlLeft: 0xA2, ControlRight: 0xA3, AltLeft: 0xA4, AltRight: 0xA5,
	Semicolon: 0xBA, Equal: 0xBB, Comma: 0xBC, Minus: 0xBD, Period: 0xBE, Slash: 0xBF, Backquote: 0xC0, BracketLeft: 0xDB, Backslash: 0xDC, BracketRight: 0xDD,
	Quote: 0xDE, IntlBackslash: 0xE2,
};
for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) { VK['Digit' + i] = 0x30 + i; VK['Numpad' + i] = 0x60 + i; }
for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) VK['Key' + String.fromCharCode(65 + i)] = 0x41 + i;
for (let i = 1; i <= 24; i++) VK['F' + i] = 0x6F + i;

// ?dbg=1: draw isolation for hunting rendering artifacts. The GPU worker runs only the first N draws of each frame (N in the input block, -1 = all) and
// reports the pipeline of the last one it ran. Find the smallest N at which the artifact appears: that draw paints it. F1/F2: N -1/+1, F3/F4: N -25/+25,
// F5: back to all draws, F6: write the current draw to the server log (the log is what to send).
function installDebugPanel(memory, block) {
	const dbg = new Int32Array(memory.buffer, block + 424, 2);
	const panel = document.createElement('div');
	panel.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;left:0;top:0;right:0;background:#000c;color:#8f8;font:12px monospace;padding:4px 8px;z-index:9;white-space:pre-wrap;pointer-events:none';
	document.body.appendChild(panel);
	let last = '', total = 0;
	new BroadcastChannel('game-debug').onmessage = (ev) => { last = ev.data.last; total = ev.data.total; render(); };
	const render = () => { const n = Atomics.load(dbg, 0); panel.textContent = 'DRAW ISOLATION  limit ' + (n < 0 ? 'off (all)' : n) + ' of ' + total + ' draws/frame   [F1 -1  F2 +1  F3 -25  F4 +25  F5 all  F6 log]\nlast draw run: ' + (last || '-'); };
	addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
		const step = { F1: -1, F2: 1, F3: -25, F4: 25 }[e.code];
		if (step !== undefined) {
			const n = Atomics.load(dbg, 0);
			Atomics.store(dbg, 0, Math.max(0, Math.min(total, (n < 0 ? total : n) + step)));
			e.preventDefault(); render();
		} else if (e.code === 'F5' && !e.ctrlKey) { Atomics.store(dbg, 0, -1); e.preventDefault(); render(); }
		else if (e.code === 'F6') { postLog('[debug draw] limit ' + Atomics.load(dbg, 0) + ' of ' + total + ' | ' + last); e.preventDefault(); }
	}, { capture: true });
	render();
}

function installInput(memory, block) {
	if (q.get('dbg')) installDebugPanel(memory, block);
	const keys = new Uint8Array(memory.buffer, block, 256);
	const w = new Int32Array(memory.buffer, block + 256, 10);
	const U16 = new Uint16Array(memory.buffer, block + 256 + 40, 64);
	const MX = 0, MY = 1, DX = 2, DY = 3, DZ = 4, BTN = 5, FOCUS = 6, LOCKED = 7, HEAD = 8, TAIL = 9;
	let W = startSize[0], H = startSize[1];		// back buffer size in device pixels (mouse clamping); updated when the window is resized
	const ext = new Int32Array(memory.buffer, block + 424, 4);		// dbgDrawLimit, dbgFrameDraws, winW, winH
	startModeWord = new Int32Array(memory.buffer, block + 440, 1);		// startMode (the start menu above)
	if (startMode) Atomics.store(startModeWord, 0, startMode);
	Atomics.store(ext, 2, W); Atomics.store(ext, 3, H);
	if (!q.get('fixed')) {
		let timer = 0;
		addEventListener('resize', () => {
			clearTimeout(timer);
			timer = setTimeout(() => {
				const [w, h] = wantSize();
				if (w === W && h === H) return;
				W = w; H = h;
				Atomics.store(ext, 2, w); Atomics.store(ext, 3, h);
				trace('window resized: back buffer ' + w + 'x' + h);
			}, 150);
		});
	}
	Atomics.store(w, FOCUS, document.hasFocus() ? 1 : 0);
	Atomics.store(w, MX, W >> 1);
	Atomics.store(w, MY, H >> 1);

	// Input tracing: the first events the page sees and where they go (POST /log), so a session that loses its keyboard says at which stage
	let traced = 0;
	const trace = (msg) => { if (traced < 80) { traced++; postLog('[page input] ' + msg); } };
	const passThrough = (e) => e.code === 'F12' || e.code === 'F11' || (e.ctrlKey && e.shiftKey && (e.code === 'KeyI' || e.code === 'KeyJ')) || e.code === 'F5';
	// Aircraft and submarine pitch and roll are on the numpad in the game (8/2/4/6, target select 7/9), which many keyboards lack. The numpad keys are bound to nothing else, so I/K (pitch), J/L (roll)
	// and U/O (target left/right) press the same keys for the engine; the real numpad keeps working.
	const FLY_ALIAS = { KeyI: 'Numpad8', KeyK: 'Numpad2', KeyJ: 'Numpad4', KeyL: 'Numpad6', KeyU: 'Numpad7', KeyO: 'Numpad9' };
	const held = new Set();
	const syncFlyAlias = () => { for (const [src, target] of Object.entries(FLY_ALIAS)) Atomics.store(keys, VK[target], held.has(src) || held.has(target) ? 0x80 : 0); };
	addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
		if (passThrough(e)) return;
		e.preventDefault();
		const vk = VK[e.code];
		if (!e.repeat) trace('keydown ' + e.code + ' vk=' + (vk || 'UNMAPPED') + ' hasFocus=' + document.hasFocus() + ' engineFocus=' + Atomics.load(w, FOCUS));
		if (vk) Atomics.store(keys, vk, 0x80);
		held.add(e.code); syncFlyAlias();
		if (e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey) {
			const head = Atomics.load(w, HEAD);
			if (((head - Atomics.load(w, TAIL)) >>> 0) < 64) { U16[head & 63] = e.key.charCodeAt(0); Atomics.store(w, HEAD, (head + 1) | 0); }
		} else if (e.code === 'Backspace' || e.code === 'Enter') {
			const head = Atomics.load(w, HEAD);
			if (((head - Atomics.load(w, TAIL)) >>> 0) < 64) { U16[head & 63] = e.code === 'Enter' ? 13 : 8; Atomics.store(w, HEAD, (head + 1) | 0); }
		}
	});
	addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
		if (passThrough(e)) return;
		e.preventDefault();
		const vk = VK[e.code];
		if (vk) Atomics.store(keys, vk, 0);
		held.delete(e.code); syncFlyAlias();
	});
	const releaseAll = () => { held.clear(); for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) Atomics.store(keys, i, 0); Atomics.store(w, BTN, 0); };
	addEventListener('focus', () => { trace('window focus'); Atomics.store(w, FOCUS, 1); });
	addEventListener('blur', () => { trace('window blur (all keys released)'); Atomics.store(w, FOCUS, 0); releaseAll(); });

	const locked = () => document.pointerLockElement === canvas;
	canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
		if (!locked()) { canvas.requestPointerLock({ unadjustedMovement: true }).catch(() => canvas.requestPointerLock()); }
		Atomics.or(w, BTN, 1 << [0, 2, 1, 3, 4][e.button]);
		e.preventDefault();
	});
	addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => { Atomics.and(w, BTN, ~(1 << [0, 2, 1, 3, 4][e.button])); });
	addEventListener('contextmenu', (e) => e.preventDefault());
	document.addEventListener('pointerlockchange', () => { trace('pointer lock ' + (locked() ? 'on' : 'off')); Atomics.store(w, LOCKED, locked() ? 1 : 0); });
	addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
		const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
		if (locked()) {
			Atomics.add(w, DX, e.movementX);
			Atomics.add(w, DY, e.movementY);
			const x = Math.max(0, Math.min(W - 1, Atomics.load(w, MX) + e.movementX));
			const y = Math.max(0, Math.min(H - 1, Atomics.load(w, MY) + e.movementY));
			Atomics.store(w, MX, x);
			Atomics.store(w, MY, y);
		} else {
			const x = Math.max(0, Math.min(W - 1, Math.round((e.clientX - r.left) * W / r.width)));
			const y = Math.max(0, Math.min(H - 1, Math.round((e.clientY - r.top) * H / r.height)));
			Atomics.add(w, DX, x - Atomics.load(w, MX));
			Atomics.add(w, DY, y - Atomics.load(w, MY));
			Atomics.store(w, MX, x);
			Atomics.store(w, MY, y);
		}
	});
	// Notches from the real scroll distance: Chrome reports ~100 px per notch on Windows (pixel mode), 3 lines in line mode; fractions (trackpads, smooth
	// scrolling) accumulate. The engine consumes one notch per frame (browser_input_wasm.cpp), so a long scroll keeps scrolling for a few frames.
	let wheelAccum = 0;
	addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {
		e.preventDefault();
		wheelAccum += -e.deltaY / (e.deltaMode === 0 ? 100 : e.deltaMode === 1 ? 3 : 1);
		const n = Math.trunc(wheelAccum);
		if (n) { wheelAccum -= n; Atomics.add(w, DZ, n); }
	}, { passive: false });
}
</script>
</body>
</html>



================================================
FILE: inspect_assets.py

"""Focused platform/provenance evidence; no archive extraction or decompilation."""
import collections, difflib, json, re, struct
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
DATA = ROOT / 'mirror/playgta5.com/data'
FILES = json.loads((ROOT / 'data-manifest.json').read_text())['files']

def main():
    headers = []
    counts = collections.Counter()
    extbytes = collections.Counter()
    for name, size, *_ in FILES:
        extbytes[Path(name).suffix] += size
        if name.endswith('.rpf'):
            with (DATA / name).open('rb') as source:
                raw = source.read(16)
            magic, entries, names_bytes, encryption = struct.unpack('<4I', raw)
            counts[(raw[:4].hex(), hex(encryption))] += 1
            headers.append({'path': name, 'bytes': size, 'header_hex': raw.hex(), 'magic_le': hex(magic),
                            'entries': entries, 'names_bytes': names_bytes, 'encryption_le': hex(encryption)})
    startup = (DATA / 'x64/data/startup.ymt').read_bytes()
    pc_marker = b'platform:/data/cdimages/scaleform_platform_pc.rpf'
    shader_info = []
    for name in ['common/shaders/win32_40/adaptiveDof.fxc', 'common/shaders/win32_nvstereo/default.fxc']:
        body = (DATA / name).read_bytes()
        off = body.find(b'DXBC')
        chunk_count = struct.unpack_from('<I', body, off + 28)[0]
        shader_models = []
        for i in range(chunk_count):
            rel = struct.unpack_from('<I', body, off + 32 + 4 * i)[0]
            kind = body[off + rel:off + rel + 4]
            if kind in [b'SHDR', b'SHEX']:
                token = struct.unpack_from('<I', body, off + rel + 8)[0]
                shader_models.append({'kind': kind.decode(), 'offset': off + rel + 8, 'token': hex(token),
                                      'major': (token >> 4) & 15, 'minor': token & 15, 'stage': token >> 16})
        compilers = [{'offset': m.start(), 'text': m.group().decode()}
                     for m in re.finditer(rb'Microsoft \(R\) HLSL Shader Compiler [0-9.]+', body)]
        shader_info.append({'path': name, 'bytes': len(body), 'dxbc_count': body.count(b'DXBC'),
                            'first_dxbc_offset': off, 'first_container_shader_models': shader_models,
                            'compiler_strings': compilers})
    resource_info = []
    for name, *_ in FILES:
        if Path(name).suffix in ['.ytd', '.ydd'] and len(resource_info) < 6:
            with (DATA / name).open('rb') as source:
                raw = source.read(16)
            resource_info.append({'path': name, 'header_hex': raw.hex(),
                                  'resource_version_le': struct.unpack_from('<I', raw, 4)[0]})
    before = (DATA / 'common/data/levels/env_test/images.meta.orig').read_text().splitlines()
    after = (DATA / 'common/data/levels/env_test/images.meta').read_text().splitlines()
    report = {'rpf_headers': headers, 'rpf_header_counts': [
        {'magic_bytes': k[0], 'encryption': k[1], 'count': n} for k, n in counts.items()],
        'bytes_by_extension': dict(extbytes),
        'startup_pc_marker': {'path': 'x64/data/startup.ymt', 'offset': startup.find(pc_marker), 'text': pc_marker.decode()},
        'shader_samples': shader_info, 'resource_samples': resource_info,
        'version_txt': (DATA / 'common/data/version.txt').read_text(),
        'dlc_actual_paths': [f[0] for f in FILES if 'dlcpacks' in f[0].lower()],
        'movie_paths': [f[0] for f in FILES if f[0].lower().endswith(('.bik', '.bk2', '.mp4'))],
        'env_test_florida_paths': [f[0] for f in FILES if 'env_test' in f[0] and 'florida' in f[0].lower()],
        'env_test_mount_list': {'original_lines': len(before), 'current_lines': len(after),
             'diff': list(difflib.unified_diff(before, after, fromfile='images.meta.orig', tofile='images.meta'))},
        'scope': '16 bytes per RPF; two shader containers; six resource headers; selected metadata. No archive decryption.'}
    (ROOT / 'snapshot/asset-evidence.json').write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    print('RPF:', report['rpf_header_counts'])
    print('PC marker:', report['startup_pc_marker'])
    print('Shader compiler:', shader_info[0]['compiler_strings'][0])
    print('Version and mount-list evidence saved to snapshot/asset-evidence.json')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()



================================================
FILE: io_worker.js
